<script>
        // Simple logic for the checkbox toggle
        document.getElementById('same_location').addEventListener('change', function(e) {
            const meetingContainer = document.getElementById('meeting_address_container');
            const meetingInput = document.getElementById('meeting_address');
            
            if(e.target.checked) {
                meetingContainer.classList.add('opacity-50');
                meetingInput.disabled = true;
                meetingInput.value = document.getElementById('event_address').value;
            } else {
                meetingContainer.classList.remove('opacity-50');
                meetingInput.disabled = false;
                meetingInput.value = '';
            }
        });

        // Sync event address to meeting address if checked
        document.getElementById('event_address').addEventListener('input', function(e) {
             if(document.getElementById('same_location').checked) {
                 document.getElementById('meeting_address').value = e.target.value;
             }
        });
    </script>

<div>
<h2 class="font-headline text-2xl font-bold text-on-surface mb-1">Construction Debris in Wetland Buffer Zone</h2>
<p class="text-on-surface-variant text-sm flex items-center gap-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-base">location_on</span>
                                    142 Lakeside Drive, Kesbewa
                                </p>
</div>
<span class="inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-error-container text-on-error-container">
                                High Priority
                            </span>
</div>
<div class="prose text-on-surface text-sm max-w-none mt-4">
<p>Reporter noted significant dumping of concrete rubble, rebar, and drywall offcuts within 5 meters of the protected wetland margin. Observed tracks indicating heavy truck access from the unpaved side road. Potential risk of runoff affecting local water quality if heavy rains occur.</p>
</div>

<script>
        // Simple script to toggle false options based on radio selection
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            const falseRadio = document.getElementById('false-status-radio');
            const otherRadios = document.querySelectorAll('input[name="status"]:not(#false-status-radio)');
            const falseOptions = document.getElementById('false-options');

            function toggleOptions() {
                if (falseRadio.checked) {
                    falseOptions.classList.remove('hidden');
                } else {
                    falseOptions.classList.add('hidden');
                }
            }

            falseRadio.addEventListener('change', toggleOptions);
            otherRadios.forEach(radio => radio.addEventListener('change', toggleOptions));
        });
    </script>
</body></html>

<!-- Description Card -->

<h2 class="font-section-title text-section-title text-on-surface mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary" data-icon="subject">subject</span>
                        Description
                    </h2>
<p class="font-body-lg text-body-lg text-on-surface-variant whitespace-pre-line">
                        Large pile of construction waste including concrete blocks, broken tiles, and plastic piping dumped approximately 15 meters from the main water body.
                        
                        Tire tracks suggest a heavy vehicle backed into the area recently. The debris is currently crushing native vegetation and poses a risk of runoff during the next rain.
                    </p>
</div>

<script>
        // Simple script to demonstrate the transition to the success state
        document.getElementById('submit-btn').addEventListener('click', function() {
            const btn = this;
            const originalContent = btn.innerHTML;
            
            // Submitting state
            btn.innerHTML = `<span class="material-symbols-outlined animate-spin">sync</span><span>Submitting...</span>`;
            btn.classList.add('opacity-80', 'cursor-not-allowed');
            btn.disabled = true;

            // Simulate network request
            setTimeout(() => {
                document.getElementById('submission-form').classList.add('hidden');
                document.querySelector('main > div:first-child').classList.add('hidden'); // Hide header
                document.getElementById('success-state').classList.remove('hidden');
            }, 1500);
        });
    </script>

<script>
        // Simple micro-interaction for the form input
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            const emailInput = document.getElementById('email');
            const form = document.querySelector('form');
            const submitBtn = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');

            emailInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
                if(e.target.value.includes('@') && e.target.value.includes('.')) {
                    submitBtn.classList.add('ring-2', 'ring-leaf-400', 'ring-offset-2', 'ring-offset-canvas');
                } else {
                    submitBtn.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-leaf-400', 'ring-offset-2', 'ring-offset-canvas');
                }
            });

            form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                const originalText = submitBtn.innerHTML;
                submitBtn.innerHTML = `<span class="material-symbols-outlined animate-spin" data-icon="progress_activity">progress_activity</span><span>Sending...</span>`;
                submitBtn.classList.add('opacity-80', 'cursor-wait');
                
                // Simulate network request
                setTimeout(() => {
                    submitBtn.innerHTML = `<span class="material-symbols-outlined" data-icon="check_circle">check_circle</span><span>Check your email</span>`;
                    submitBtn.classList.remove('bg-forest-800', 'hover:bg-primary-container');
                    submitBtn.classList.add('bg-leaf-400', 'text-forest-950');
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        submitBtn.innerHTML = originalText;
                        submitBtn.classList.remove('opacity-80', 'cursor-wait', 'bg-leaf-400', 'text-forest-950');
                        submitBtn.classList.add('bg-forest-800', 'hover:bg-primary-container');
                        emailInput.value = '';
                    }, 3000);
                }, 1500);
            });
        });
    </script>
</body></html>

<script>
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            const form = document.getElementById('magic-link-form');
            const emailInput = document.getElementById('email');
            const errorMsg = document.getElementById('email-error');
            const feedbackMsg = document.getElementById('feedback-message');
            const submitBtn = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');

            form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                
                // Basic validation
                if (!emailInput.value || !emailInput.value.includes('@')) {
                    emailInput.classList.add('border-error', 'focus:border-error', 'focus:ring-error');
                    emailInput.classList.remove('border-outline-variant', 'focus:border-primary', 'focus:ring-primary');
                    errorMsg.classList.remove('hidden');
                    return;
                }

                // Reset validation state
                emailInput.classList.remove('border-error', 'focus:border-error', 'focus:ring-error');
                emailInput.classList.add('border-outline-variant', 'focus:border-primary', 'focus:ring-primary');
                errorMsg.classList.add('hidden');

                // Simulate sending (UI feedback)
                submitBtn.disabled = true;
                const originalText = submitBtn.querySelector('span').innerText;
                submitBtn.querySelector('span').innerText = 'Sending...';
                submitBtn.classList.add('opacity-70');

                setTimeout(() => {
                    submitBtn.disabled = false;
                    submitBtn.querySelector('span').innerText = originalText;
                    submitBtn.classList.remove('opacity-70');
                    
                    form.classList.add('hidden');
                    feedbackMsg.classList.remove('hidden');
                    
                }, 1500);
            });
            
            // Clear error on input
            emailInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                 if(!errorMsg.classList.contains('hidden')){
                    emailInput.classList.remove('border-error', 'focus:border-error', 'focus:ring-error');
                    emailInput.classList.add('border-outline-variant', 'focus:border-primary', 'focus:ring-primary');
                    errorMsg.classList.add('hidden');
                 }
            });
        });
    </script>
</body></html>

        /* Custom toggle switch styling */
        input:checked ~ .block {
            background-color: #174c33; /* primary-container */

        input:checked ~ .dot {

<script>
        // Simple toggle logic for demo purposes
        const toggle = document.getElementById('unread-toggle');
        const readCards = document.querySelectorAll('.bg-surface-container-low\\/50, .border-dashed');
        
        toggle.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            readCards.forEach(card => {
                if (e.target.checked) {
                    card.style.display = 'none';
                } else {
                    card.style.display = 'block';
                }
            });
        });
    </script>

<h2 class="font-section-title text-section-title text-on-surface text-center mb-2">Sign out of EcoTrack?</h2>
<p class="font-body-md text-on-surface-variant text-center mb-stack-xl">You will need to sign back in to access your reports and personal workspace.</p>

                    Sign out
                </button>
<button class="w-full bg-transparent border border-outline text-on-surface rounded-full min-h-touch-target font-label-md text-label-md flex items-center justify-center transition-colors hover:bg-surface-variant active:scale-95" id="cancel-signout">

<script>
        const trigger = document.getElementById('trigger-signout');
        const overlay = document.getElementById('signout-overlay');
        const sheet = document.getElementById('signout-sheet');
        const cancel = document.getElementById('cancel-signout');

        function openSheet() {
            overlay.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
            overlay.classList.add('opacity-100', 'pointer-events-auto');
            setTimeout(() => {
                sheet.classList.remove('translate-y-full');
                sheet.classList.add('translate-y-0');
            }, 10);
        }

        function closeSheet() {
            sheet.classList.remove('translate-y-0');
            sheet.classList.add('translate-y-full');
            setTimeout(() => {
                overlay.classList.remove('opacity-100', 'pointer-events-auto');
                overlay.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
            }, 300);
        }

        trigger.addEventListener('click', openSheet);
        cancel.addEventListener('click', closeSheet);
        overlay.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            if (e.target === overlay) {
                closeSheet();
            }
        });
    </script>
</body></html>
